```
629	// ============================================================================
630	// AI_053 世纪之花触须 264(NPC.cs:32406-32536):二阶段环绕本体漂移攻击。
631	// ai[0]/ai[1] = 环绕偏移(px,每 120-480t 随机重掷 ±100);环绕半径 200,
632	// 本体 <25%/10% 血各 +100;加速度 0.2(符号翻转 0.9 阻尼),速度钳 ±8;
633	// ai[3]>0 时锚定 ai[3]-1 指定的 NPC(原版多人分割);本体消失 → 自毁。
634	// ============================================================================
635	export function planteraTentacle2AI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
636	  const l = la(e);
637	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; l[0] = 0; }
638	  const boss = (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 262) ?? null;
639	  if (!boss) { e.hp -= 9999; if (e.hp <= 0) e.dead = true; return; }
640	  // 原版 :32426-32429:ai[3]>0 → 锚 ai[3]-1 的 NPC(本框架触须不设锚,恒随本体)
641	  const anchor = e.ai3 > 0
642	    ? (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => !n.dead && n.id === e.ai3 - 1) ?? boss
643	    : boss;
644	  e.master = anchor; // 触须链渲染用（Renderer.drawPlanteraVine，Main.cs:22262-22285）
645	  // 偏移重掷(:32432-32439)
646	  l[0] -= 1;
647	  if (l[0] <= 0) {
648	    l[0] = rndRange(120, 480);
649	    e.ai0 = rndRange(-100, 101);
650	    e.ai1 = rndRange(-100, 101);
651	  }
652	  // 环绕半径随本体血量(:32442-32451);expert 动态半径 +300×(1-血比) + 加速度 0.5
653	  // (:32454-32459);getGoodWorld 加速度 +4(:32460-32463)
654	  const expertTen = mainExpertMode(game.world);
655	  const ftwTen = !!game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld;
656	  let orbit = 200;
657	  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp * 0.25) orbit += 100;
658	  if (boss.hp < boss.maxHp * 0.1) orbit += 100;
659	  if (expertTen) orbit += 300 * (1 - boss.hp / boss.maxHp);
660	  const accTen = 0.2 + (expertTen ? 0.3 : 0) + (ftwTen ? 4 : 0);
661	  // 目标点 = 锚点 + 归一化偏移×半径;步进 accTen,符号翻转 0.9 阻尼(:32467-32509)
662	  let dx = anchor.cx + e.ai0 - anchor.cx;
663	  let dy = anchor.cy + e.ai1 - anchor.cy;
664	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
665	  dx = (dx / d) * orbit;
666	  dy = (dy / d) * orbit;
667	  if (e.cx < anchor.cx + dx) { e.vx += accTen; if (e.vx < 0 && dx > 0) e.vx *= 0.9; }
668	  else if (e.cx > anchor.cx + dx) { e.vx -= accTen; if (e.vx > 0 && dx < 0) e.vx *= 0.9; }
669	  if (e.cy < anchor.cy + dy) { e.vy += accTen; if (e.vy < 0 && dy > 0) e.vy *= 0.9; }
670	  else if (e.cy > anchor.cy + dy) { e.vy -= accTen; if (e.vy > 0 && dy < 0) e.vy *= 0.9; }
671	  e.vx = Math.max(-8, Math.min(8, e.vx));
672	  e.vy = Math.max(-8, Math.min(8, e.vy));
673	  // 朝向旋转(:32526-32535)
674	  if (dx > 0) { e.facing = 1; e.spin = Math.atan2(dy, dx); }
675	  if (dx < 0) { e.facing = -1; e.spin = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + 3.14; }
676	  void player;
677	  e.x += e.vx;
678	  e.y += e.vy;
679	}
```

```
32	const baseOf = (e: Enemy) => {
33	  let b = baseStats.get(e);
34	  if (!b) { b = { dmg: e.def.damage, def: e.def.defense ?? 0 }; baseStats.set(e, b); }
35	  return b;
36	};
37	
38	/** 每实例附加槽位(原版 alpha/noTileCollide/localAI 等无对应 Enemy 字段) */
39	interface BodyX { alpha: number; noTC: boolean; dying: boolean; deadT: number }
40	interface HeadX { alpha: number; free: boolean; l0: number; l1: number }
41	interface FistX { alpha: number; noTC: boolean; state: number }
42	const bodyX = new WeakMap<Enemy, BodyX>();
43	const headX = new WeakMap<Enemy, HeadX>();
44	const fistX = new WeakMap<Enemy, FistX>();
45	const bodyXOf = (e: Enemy): BodyX => {
46	  let x = bodyX.get(e);
47	  if (!x) { x = { alpha: 255, noTC: false, dying: false, deadT: 0 }; bodyX.set(e, x); }
48	  return x;
49	};
50	const headXOf = (e: Enemy): HeadX => {
51	  let x = headX.get(e);
52	  if (!x) { x = { alpha: 255, free: false, l0: 0, l1: 0 }; headX.set(e, x); }
53	  return x;
54	};
55	const fistXOf = (e: Enemy): FistX => {
56	  let x = fistX.get(e);
57	  if (!x) { x = { alpha: 255, noTC: true, state: 0 }; fistX.set(e, x); }
58	  return x;
59	};
60	
61	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
62	// 族内查找(原版静态 golemBoss 索引的等价物:NPC.cs:6052 声明、:19449 每帧回填)
63	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
64	
65	const findBody = (game: GameHooks): Enemy | null =>
66	  (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 245) ?? null;
67	const findHead = (game: GameHooks): Enemy | null =>
68	  (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find((n) => !n.dead && (n.vanillaId === 246 || n.vanillaId === 249)) ?? null;
69	
70	/** GetMyBalance + 脱离神庙/丛林加成(NPC.cs:19450-19457 / :19173-19180 / :31274-31282 / :31506-31512):
71	 *  经典单人 = 1;getGoodWorld +2/+3(expert,略);
72	 *  (!ZoneLihzhardTemple && !ZoneJungle) || 玩家 Center.Y < worldSurface*16 → ×2。
73	 *  Zone 判定接 Game.scene(SceneMetrics):zoneTemple = 中心格墙 87(SceneMetrics.cs:688,
74	 *  神庙砖 226 不构成独立 Zone,只计入 JungleTileCount——SceneMetrics.cs:613,阈值 140)。 */
75	const balanceNum = (game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): number => {
76	  let num = 1;
77	  const s = game.scene;
78	  const inTempleOrJungle = !!s?.zoneTemple || !!s?.zoneJungle;
79	  if (!inTempleOrJungle || (player != null && player.cy < game.world.groundLevel * TILE)) num *= 2;
80	  return num;
81	};
82	
83	/** 出生渐显(SetDefaults alpha=255;AI 内 alpha -= 10/2t,NPC.cs:19477-19485 / :31310-31317 / :19187-19194)。
84	 *  返回剩余 alpha;期间 ai[1] 由调用方冻结(本体/拳 0、挂载头 30)——蓄力计时不起跑 */
85	const fadeAlpha = (e: Enemy, x: { alpha: number }): number => {
86	  if (x.alpha <= 0) return 0;
87	  x.alpha = Math.max(0, x.alpha - 10);
88	  e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(254, x.alpha);
89	  return x.alpha;
90	};
91	
92	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
93	// 碰撞(本族私有拷贝:bossAI.collideGround 是 private;140px 宽本体需多列采样防半悬空)
94	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
95	
96	/** 落地/顶墙/侧墙判定(原版共享 Collision 的 AABB 近似:底/顶三列、侧墙三点) */
97	function collideGround(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks): void {
98	  const st = game.world.store;
99	  const cols = [e.x + 2, e.cx, e.x + e.w - 2];
100	  if (e.vy >= 0) {
101	    const ty = Math.floor((e.y + e.h) / TILE);
102	    let top = Infinity;
103	    for (const cx of cols) {
104	      const tx = Math.floor(cx / TILE);
105	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) top = Math.min(top, ty * TILE);
106	    }
107	    if (top < Infinity) { e.y = top - e.h; e.vy = 0; e.onGround = true; }
108	    else e.onGround = false;
109	  } else {
110	    e.onGround = false;
111	    const ty = Math.floor(e.y / TILE);
112	    let bottom = -Infinity;
113	    for (const cx of cols) {
114	      const tx = Math.floor(cx / TILE);
115	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) bottom = Math.max(bottom, (ty + 1) * TILE);
116	    }
117	    if (bottom > -Infinity) { e.y = bottom; e.vy = 0; }
118	  }
119	  const rows = [e.y + 2, e.cy, e.y + e.h - 2];
120	  if (e.vx > 0) {
121	    const tx = Math.floor((e.x + e.w) / TILE);
122	    for (const ry of rows) {
123	      const ty = Math.floor(ry / TILE);
124	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { e.x = tx * TILE - e.w; e.vx = 0; break; }
125	    }
126	  } else if (e.vx < 0) {
127	    const tx = Math.floor(e.x / TILE);
128	    for (const ry of rows) {
129	      const ty = Math.floor(ry / TILE);
130	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { e.x = (tx + 1) * TILE; e.vx = 0; break; }
131	    }
132	  }
133	}
134	
135	/** AABB 与实心块相交(原版 Collision.SolidTiles 近似):拳冲拳段关闭穿墙后的撞停判定 */
136	function hitSolid(game: GameHooks, e: Enemy): boolean {
137	  const st = game.world.store;
138	  const x0 = Math.floor(e.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((e.x + e.w - 0.01) / TILE);
139	  const y0 = Math.floor(e.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((e.y + e.h - 0.01) / TILE);
140	  for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
141	    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
142	      if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return true;
143	    }
144	  }
145	  return false;
146	}
147	
148	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
149	// 弹幕(原版 NewProjectile 调用点:id 258 火焰弹 / 259 眼激光)
150	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
151	
152	/** 嘴部/额前火焰弹(:31329-31347 / :31430-31447 / :31596-31617):
153	 *  从 (e.center + (ox,oy)) 朝玩家中心 proj 258 */
154	function golemSpit(
155	  game: GameHooks, e: Enemy, player: Player, ox: number, oy: number,
156	  spd: number, dmg: number, projId: number,
157	): void {
158	  const sx = e.cx + ox, sy = e.cy + oy;
159	  const dx = player.cx - sx, dy = player.cy - sy;
160	  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
161	  shoot(game, sx, sy, (dx / d) * spd, (dy / d) * spd, dmg, projId);
162	}
163	
164	/** 挂载头眼激光(:31459-31484):双侧眼(±18*scale,-22*scale)各一发 11 速;
165	 *  玩家在头正上/下方(localAI[1]==0)→ 双眼齐射,否则朝向侧单眼(±30*scale)12 速;伤 28,proj 259 */
166	function golemEyeLasers(game: GameHooks, e: Enemy, player: Player, side: number): void {
167	  const fire = (ox: number, spd: number) => {
168	    const sx = e.cx + ox, sy = e.cy - 22 * SCALE;
169	    const dx = player.cx - sx, dy = player.cy - sy;
170	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
171	    const vx = (dx / d) * spd, vy = (dy / d) * spd;
172	    shoot(game, sx + vx * 3, sy + vy * 3, vx, vy, 28, 259, 300);
173	  };
174	  if (side === 0) { fire(-18 * SCALE, 11); fire(18 * SCALE, 11); }
175	  else fire(side * 30 * SCALE, 12);
176	}
177	
178	/** 自由头眼激光(:31659-31717):双眼(±14*scale,-50*scale)各一发 proj 259;
179	 *  伤 24/速 11,本体(245)残血每降一档 +1 伤/+0.25 速(调用方传本体血量比值 r);
180	 *  无视线(flag49)→ 伤 ×1.5、速 ×2.5、按玩家速度随机提前量瞄准 */
181	function golemEyeLasersFree(
182	  game: GameHooks, e: Enemy, player: Player, noLos: boolean, r: number,
183	): void {
184	  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
185	    const sx = e.cx + (i === 0 ? -14 : 14) * SCALE;
186	    const sy = e.cy - 50 * SCALE;
187	    let dmg = 24, spd = 11;
188	    if (r < 0.5) { dmg += 1; spd += 0.25; }
189	    if (r < 0.4) { dmg += 1; spd += 0.25; }
190	    if (r < 0.3) { dmg += 1; spd += 0.25; }
191	    if (r < 0.2) { dmg += 1; spd += 0.25; }
192	    if (r < 0.1) { dmg += 1; spd += 0.25; }
193	    let tx = player.cx, ty = player.cy;
194	    if (noLos) {
195	      dmg = Math.floor(dmg * 1.5);
196	      spd *= 2.5;
197	      const pvx = (player as unknown as { vx: number }).vx ?? 0;
198	      const pvy = (player as unknown as { vy: number }).vy ?? 0;
199	      tx += pvx * Math.random() * 50;
200	      ty += pvy * Math.random() * 50;
201	    }
202	    const dx = tx - sx, dy = ty - sy;
203	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
204	    const vx = (dx / d) * spd, vy = (dy / d) * spd;
205	    shoot(game, sx + vx * 3, sy + vy * 3, vx, vy, dmg, 259, 300);
206	  }
207	}
208	
209	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
210	// AI_045 石巨人本体 245(NPC.cs:19447-19722)
211	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
212	
213	/** 挂载头 → 自由态(原版 HitEffect :85842-85847:246 血尽原地 NewNPC 249;
214	 *  本工程头原体变身,满血+属性对齐 249 SetDefaults :12139-12149) */
215	function golemHeadGoFree(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks): void {
216	  const x = headXOf(e);
217	  if (x.free) return;
218	  x.free = true;
219	  e.ai3 = 1;          // 自由态标记(探针/部件锚定可观测)
220	  e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
221	  e.hp = e.maxHp;     // 原版 249 满血登场
222	  game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#C04030', 16, 2.6, { life: 30 });
223	  game.playSfx('roar', 0.8);
224	}
225	
226	export function golemAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
227	  const x = bodyXOf(e);
228	
229	  // ---- 血尽转换(首行):死亡演出 60t 后真死;头由自身 AI 转自由态续战 ----
230	  if (e.hp <= 0 && !x.dying) {
231	    x.dying = true; x.deadT = 0;
232	    e.ai3 = 1;          // 死亡标记(头据此转自由态)
233	    e.hp = e.maxHp;     // 满血续命防每 tick 重入
234	    game.playSfx('roar', 0.9);
235	  }
236	
237	  // ---- 首帧:localAI[0]==0 生成部件(NPC.cs:19458-19467) ----
238	  //  原版 NewNPC 传左上角坐标:拳 247=(Center-84,-9)、248=(+78,-9)、头 246=(-3,-57)
239	  if (!e.bInit) {
240	    e.bInit = true;
241	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
242	    e.alphaFade = false;           // 接管渐显(原版 AI 内 alpha -= 10)
243	    const spawn = (id: number, ox: number, oy: number, side: number) => {
244	      const p = spawnPart(game, e, id);
245	      if (!p) return;
246	      p.x = e.cx + ox; p.y = e.cy + oy;
247	      p.ai0 = side;                // 拳 ai0=侧别(-1 左/1 右;头不使用)
248	    };
249	    spawn(247, -84, -9, -1);
250	    spawn(248, 78, -9, 1);
251	    spawn(246, -3, -57, 0);
252	  }
253	
254	  // ---- 出生渐显(NPC.cs:19477-19485):期间 ai[1]=0,蓄力冻结 ----
255	  if (fadeAlpha(e, x) > 0) e.ai1 = 0;
256	
257	  // ---- 死亡演出:无敌+碎裂尘,60t 后真死(头/拳继续) ----
258	  if (x.dying) {
259	    e.iframes = 2;
260	    e.vx *= 0.9;
261	    e.vy = Math.min(e.vy + GRAV, MAX_FALL);
262	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
263	    collideGround(e, game);
264	    x.deadT++;
265	    if (x.deadT % 6 === 0) {
266	      game.spawnParticles(
267	        e.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * e.w, e.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * e.h,
268	        '#B04030', 6, 2.4, { life: 36 });
269	    }
270	    if (x.deadT >= 60) { e.dead = true; return; }
271	    return;
272	  }
273	
274	  const num = balanceNum(game, player);
275	
276	  // ---- 部件存活扫描(NPC.cs:19487-19512):flag=头/flag2=左拳/flag3=右拳 ----
277	  //  注意头原体变身自由态后(ai3=1)视为"挂载头已失"——本体解除无敌且蓄力提速,
278	  //  对齐原版"246 死亡 → NewNPC 249"后 flag 为假的语义
279	  let hasHead = false, hasL = false, hasR = false;
280	  for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
281	    if (n.dead) continue;
282	    if (n.vanillaId === 246 && n.ai3 !== 1) hasHead = true;
283	    else if (n.vanillaId === 247) hasL = true;
284	    else if (n.vanillaId === 248) hasR = true;
285	  }
286	  // dontTakeDamage = 挂载头在场(NPC.cs:19513):一阶段本体无敌,先打头/拳
287	  if (hasHead) e.iframes = 2;
288	  // getGoodWorld 落体砸火把(:19514-19534,expert 略)
289	
290	  // ---- 断臂尘(NPC.cs:19536-19595):原版每 tick 双簇 Dust+Gore,此处每 10t 一簇粒子近似 ----
291	  if (!hasL && e.aiT % 10 === 0) game.spawnParticles(e.cx - 80 * SCALE, e.cy - 9, '#9A8FA0', 3, 1.2, { life: 40 });
292	  if (!hasR && e.aiT % 10 === 0) game.spawnParticles(e.cx + 62 * SCALE, e.cy - 9, '#9A8FA0', 3, 1.2, { life: 40 });
293	
294	  // ---- 跳跃穿墙解除(NPC.cs:19569-19578):下落低于玩家顶 / 有视线且自身不在块内 ----
295	  if (x.noTC && player && !player.dead) {
296	    if (e.vy > 0 && e.y + e.h > player.y) x.noTC = false;
297	    else if (losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy) && !hitSolid(game, e)) x.noTC = false;
298	  }
299	
300	  // TargetClosest 的 direction 近似:朝玩家符号,同侧回退 facing
301	  const dir = player ? (Math.sign(player.cx - e.cx) || e.facing || 1) : 1;
302	  if (player) e.facing = dir;
303	
304	  if (player && e.ai0 === 0) {
305	    // ---- 地面蓄力(NPC.cs:19580-19645):ai[1] 攒 300 → -20 蹲伏 20t → ==-1 起跳 ----
306	    //  蹲伏段(负值)num4 固定 1;蓄力段按断臂/残血提速,再乘脱庙狂暴系数
307	    if (e.vy === 0) {
308	      e.vx *= 0.8;
309	      let num4 = 1;
310	      if (e.ai1 > 0) {
311	        if (!hasL) num4 += 2;
312	        if (!hasR) num4 += 2;
313	        if (!hasHead) num4 += 2;
314	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp) num4 += 1;
315	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) num4 += 4;
316	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 3) num4 += 8;
317	        num4 *= num;
318	        // getGoodWorld:num4 += 100(:19613-19616,expert 略)
319	      }
320	      e.ai1 += num4;
321	      if (e.ai1 >= 300) e.ai1 = -20;
322	      else if (e.ai1 === -1) {
323	        // 起跳(:19624-19643):穿墙跳,vx=4*dir;残血跳更高 -12.1*(num+9)/10 钳 -19.1
324	        x.noTC = true;
325	        e.vx = 4 * dir;
326	        e.vy = e.hp < e.maxHp ? Math.max(-19.1, (-12.1 * (num + 9)) / 10) : -12.1;
327	        e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0;
328	      }
329	    }
330	  } else if (player && e.ai0 === 1) {
331	    // ---- 跳跃段(NPC.cs:19646-19708) ----
332	    if (e.vy === 0) {
333	      // 落地(:19648-19661):Item14 音效 + 落地尘/Gore
334	      game.playSfx('roar', 0.35);
335	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.y + e.h, '#9A8FA0', 12, 2.2, { life: 40 });
336	      e.ai0 = 0;
337	    } else {
338	      // 玩家在身下水平跨度内 → 收横向速度(夹住玩家下压),否则朝玩家加速钳 ±num7
339	      if (e.x < player.x + player.w && e.x + e.w > player.x) {
340	        e.vx *= 0.9;
341	        if (e.y + e.h < player.y) e.vy += (0.2 * (num + 1)) / 2;
342	      } else {
343	        e.vx += 0.2 * dir;
344	        let num7 = 3;
345	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp) num7 += 1;
346	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) num7 += 1;
347	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) num7 += 1;
348	        num7 *= (num + 1) / 2;
349	        e.vx = clamp(e.vx, -num7, num7);
350	      }
351	    }
352	  }
353	
354	  // ---- 玩家死亡 vs 远离（NPC.cs:19469-19475 / 19709-19721）----
355	  // 玩家死：仅 noTileCollide=true（无 despawn 无状态切换，AI 继续跑）；
356	  // 活人曼哈顿 >3000：瞬消（二次判定 TargetClosest 后）
357	  if (!player) {
358	    x.noTC = true;
359	  } else if (Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) + Math.abs(e.cy - player.cy) > 3000) {
360	    e.dead = true; e.bossFled = true;
361	    return;
362	  }
363	
364	  // ---- 重力+位移+落地(原版共享物理:noGravity=false → 0.3/2t、maxFall 10) ----
365	  e.vy = Math.min(e.vy + GRAV, MAX_FALL);
366	  e.x += e.vx;
367	  e.y += e.vy;
368	  if (!x.noTC) collideGround(e, game);
369	  else e.onGround = false;
370	}
371	
372	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
373	// aiStyle 46 挂载头 246(NPC.cs:31273-31499)+ aiStyle 48 自由头 249(NPC.cs:31500-31741)
374	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
375	
376	export function golemHeadAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
377	  const x = headXOf(e);
378	  if (e.vanillaId === 249) x.free = true;   // 直接生成的自由头(原版 NewNPC 249)
379	  if (!e.bInit) {
380	    e.bInit = true;
381	    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0;
382	    e.ai3 = x.free ? 1 : 0;
383	    e.alphaFade = false;
384	    x.alpha = x.free ? 0 : 255;             // 249 SetDefaults 无 alpha(:12139-12149)
385	  }
386	
387	  // ---- 血尽转换(首行):自由头死=战斗结束全族自毁;挂载头死=转自由态 ----
388	  if (e.hp <= 0) {
389	    if (x.free) {
390	      e.dead = true;
391	      for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
392	        if (!n.dead && n !== e && n.vanillaId !== null && n.vanillaId >= 245 && n.vanillaId <= 249) n.dead = true;
393	      }
394	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#C04030', 20, 3, { life: 40 });
395	      game.playSfx('roar', 1);
396	      return;
397	    }
398	    golemHeadGoFree(e, game);
399	  }
400	
401	  // ---- 属性:自由头按 249 SetDefaults(NPC.cs:12139-12149:damage 80/defense 32) ----
402	  if (x.free) {
403	    const free = vanillaNpc(249);
404	    e.def.damage = free?.damage ?? 80;
405	    e.def.defense = free?.defense ?? 32;
406	  } else {
407	    const b = baseOf(e);
408	    e.def.damage = b.dmg;
409	    e.def.defense = b.def;
410	  }
411	
412	  // 本体死亡/缺失 → 挂载头转自由态(本体死透后头继续)
413	  const body = findBody(game);
414	  if (!x.free && (!body || body.ai3 === 1 || body.hp <= 0)) golemHeadGoFree(e, game);
415	
416	  const num = balanceNum(game, player);
417	
418	  if (!x.free && body) {
419	    // ================= 挂载态 aiStyle 46(NPC.cs:31273-31499) =================
420	    // noTileCollide = true(:31284);锚点 = 本体.Center + (-3*scale,-57*scale)(:31290-31308)
421	    const dx = body.cx - 3 * SCALE - e.cx;
422	    const dy = body.cy - 57 * SCALE - e.cy;
423	    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
424	    if (dist < 100) { e.spin = 0; e.vx = dx; e.vy = dy; }        // 100 内整帧贴锚
425	    else { e.vx = (dx / dist) * 100; e.vy = (dy / dist) * 100; e.spin = e.vx * 0.1; }
426	    // 渐显(:31310-31317):期间 ai[1]=30
427	    if (fadeAlpha(e, x) > 0) e.ai1 = 30;
428	
429	    if (player) {
430	      if (e.ai0 === 0) {
431	        // ---- 嘴部火焰弹循环(:31319-31347):300t 一发 proj 258(伤 18 速 8) ----
432	        e.ai1 += 1;
433	        if (e.ai1 < 20 || e.ai1 > 280) { e.ai1 += (2 * (num - 1)) / 3; x.l0 = 1; }  // 张嘴帧
434	        else { e.ai1 += (1 * (num - 1)) / 2; x.l0 = 0; }
435	        if (e.ai1 >= 300) {
436	          e.ai1 = 0;
437	          golemSpit(game, e, player, 0, 10 * SCALE, 8, 18, 258);
438	        }
439	      } else if (e.ai0 === 1) {
440	        // ---- 眼部阶段(:31353-31486):嘴弹伤 24 + 眼激光 259(伤 28) ----
441	        x.l1 = player.cx < e.cx - e.w ? -1 : player.cx > e.cx + e.w ? 1 : 0;        // localAI[1]
442	        const num733 = (num + 3) / 4;
443	        e.ai1 += num733;
444	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.4) e.ai1 += num733;
445	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.2) e.ai1 += num733;
446	        x.l0 = e.ai1 < 20 || e.ai1 > 280 ? 1 : 0;
447	        if (e.ai1 >= 300) {
448	          e.ai1 = 0;
449	          golemSpit(game, e, player, x.l1 * 40 * SCALE, 10 * SCALE, 8, 24, 258);
450	        }
451	        e.ai2 += num733;
452	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 3) e.ai2 += num733;
453	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) e.ai2 += num733;
454	        if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 5) e.ai2 += num733;
455	        if (!losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy)) e.ai2 += 4;          // :31456 无视线催速
456	        if (e.ai2 > 60 + Math.random() * 600) {
457	          e.ai2 = 0;
458	          golemEyeLasers(game, e, player, x.l1);
459	        }
460	      }
461	      // 阶段派生(:31487-31491):血 < 半 → 眼部阶段
462	      e.ai0 = e.hp < e.maxHp / 2 ? 1 : 0;
463	    }
464	  } else {
465	    // ================= 自由态 aiStyle 48 GolemHeadFree(NPC.cs:31500-31741) =================
466	    // flag48(:31503-31515):头自身看不到玩家 → 穿墙 + 火焰弹不蓄满(头↔玩家视线)
467	    const rBody = findBody(game);
468	    const noLos = !player || !losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy);
469	    // flag49(:31646-31651):本体↔玩家无视线 → 激光催速 + 弹道预判;
470	    // 本体已亡(本移植死亡链)→ 退化为头↔玩家视线
471	    const noLosBody = rBody
472	      ? !player || !losClear(game, rBody.cx, rBody.cy, player.cx, player.cy)
473	      : noLos;
474	    if (player) {
475	      // 悬浮(:31521-31564):目标 = 玩家 + (0,-300),7 速/0.05 步进(符号翻转双步进)
476	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - 300 - e.cy;
477	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
478	      e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (dx / d) * 7, 0.05);
479	      e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (dy / d) * 7, 0.05);
480	      // 火焰弹节奏(:31566-31595):num761=(num+4)/5,分档读本体(golemBoss=245)残血(:31574-31594)。
481	      // 本体已亡(本移植死亡链,原版无此态)→ 比值 0 = 全档,等价原版读尸体槽 life<=0 恒真
482	      const num761 = (num + 4) / 5;
483	      e.ai1 += num761;
484	      const r = rBody ? rBody.hp / rBody.maxHp : 0;
485	      if (r < 0.8) e.ai1 += num761;
486	      if (r < 0.6) e.ai1 += num761;
487	      if (r < 0.2) e.ai1 += num761;
488	      if (r < 0.1) e.ai1 += num761;
489	      x.l0 = e.ai1 < 20 || e.ai1 > 280 ? 1 : 0;   // 张嘴帧(:31584-31592)
490	      if (noLos) e.ai1 = 20;                       // :31593-31595 无视线时不蓄满
491	      if (e.ai1 >= 300) {                          // :31596-31617 嘴弹 proj 258(伤 20 速 8)
492	        e.ai1 = 0;
493	        golemSpit(game, e, player, 0, -10 * SCALE, 8, 20, 258);
494	      }
495	      // 眼激光节奏(:31618-31658):基数 num,分档读本体残血(:31626-31645;r 与上方火焰弹
496	      // 同源);激光伤/速分档(:31683-31705,阈值 0.5/0.4/0.3/0.2/0.1)经 golemEyeLasersFree 传 r
497	      e.ai2 += num;
498	      if (r < 1 / 1.25) e.ai2 += num;
499	      if (r < 1 / 1.5) e.ai2 += num;
500	      if (r < 1 / 2) e.ai2 += num;
501	      if (r < 1 / 3) e.ai2 += num;
502	      if (r < 1 / 4) e.ai2 += num;
503	      if (r < 1 / 5) e.ai2 += num;
504	      if (r < 1 / 6) e.ai2 += num;
505	      if (noLosBody) e.ai2 += num * 10;            // :31646-31651 flag49 催速
506	      if (e.ai2 > 100 + Math.random() * 4800) {
507	        e.ai2 = 0;
508	        golemEyeLasersFree(game, e, player, noLosBody, r);
509	      }
510	    }
511	    // :31721-31740 排焰尘(纯视觉,略)
512	  }
513	
514	  e.x += e.vx;
515	  e.y += e.vy;
516	}
517	
518	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
519	// AI_047 石巨人拳 247/248(NPC.cs:19171-19446)
520	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
521	// 原版 ai[0] 是状态机(0 归位/1 蓄力/2 冲拳/3 直飞);任务约定 ai0=侧别,
522	// 状态机落在本文件 WeakMap(fistX.state),计时仍用 ai[1]。
523	
524	export function golemFistAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
525	  const x = fistXOf(e);
526	  if (!e.bInit) {
527	    e.bInit = true;
528	    e.ai0 = e.vanillaId === 247 ? -1 : 1;   // ai0=侧别(-1 左拳/1 右拳;由本体生成时预置)
529	    e.ai1 = 0;
530	    e.alphaFade = false;
531	    x.alpha = 255;
532	  }
533	  const isLeft = e.vanillaId === 247;
534	
535	  // ---- 锚主:golemBoss 缺失 → 自毁(NPC.cs:19182-19186) ----
536	  //  本体存活(含死亡演出)→ 锚本体;本体死透 → 锚自由头(任务约定"锚定本体(或头)")
537	  const body = findBody(game);
538	  const head = findHead(game);
539	  const anchor = body && !body.dead ? body : head;
540	  if (!anchor) { e.dead = true; return; }
541	  e.master = anchor; // 拳链渲染用（Renderer.drawGolemFistChain，Main.cs:22593-22632）
542	
543	  const num = balanceNum(game, player);
544	  // 渐显(:19187-19194):期间 ai[1]=0
545	  if (fadeAlpha(e, x) > 0) e.ai1 = 0;
546	
547	  // ---- 肩锚点(:19197-19199):锚主.Center + 锚主.velocity + (±84/78*scale, -9*scale) ----
548	  const offX = isLeft ? -84 : 78;
549	  const tx = anchor.cx + anchor.vx + offX * SCALE;
550	  const ty = anchor.cy + anchor.vy - 9 * SCALE;
551	  const dx = tx - e.cx, dy = ty - e.cy;
552	  const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
553	  e.facing = isLeft ? 1 : -1;               // 镜像朝向(原版贴图方向)
554	
555	  if (!player) { e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy; return; }
556	
557	  if (x.state === 0) {
558	    // ---- 归位/待机蓄力(:19202-19270) ----
559	    x.noTC = true;
560	    let num3 = 14;
561	    if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) num3 += 3;
562	    if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) num3 += 3;
563	    if (anchor.hp < anchor.maxHp) num3 += 8;
564	    num3 *= (num + 3) / 4;
565	    if (num3 > 32) num3 = 32;
566	    if (dist < 12 + num3) {
567	      // 贴肩:整帧速度 = 偏移量(原版 velocity = delta),蓄力计时(:19216-19259)
568	      e.spin = 0;
569	      e.vx = dx; e.vy = dy;
570	      e.ai1 += num;
571	      if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) e.ai1 += num;
572	      if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) e.ai1 += num;
573	      if (anchor.hp < anchor.maxHp) e.ai1 += 10 * num;
574	      if (e.ai1 >= 60) {
575	        // 出拳侧别门(:19244-19256):左拳只在玩家偏左时出、右拳对称
576	        const sideOk = isLeft ? e.cx + 100 > player.cx : e.cx - 100 < player.cx;
577	        e.ai1 = 0;
578	        if (sideOk) x.state = 1;
579	      }
580	    } else {
581	      e.vx = (dx / dist) * num3;
582	      e.vy = (dy / dist) * num3;
583	      // 归位朝向(:19260-19262):默认 atan2(-vy,-vx),247 镜像为 atan2(vy,vx)
584	      e.spin = isLeft ? Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) : Math.atan2(-e.vy, -e.vx);
585	    }
586	  } else if (x.state === 1) {
587	    // ---- 蓄力:贴肩 30t(:19271-19332) ----
588	    e.ai1 += 1;
589	    e.x = tx - e.w / 2;                     // 原版 base.Center = vector(整帧钉在肩锚)
590	    e.y = ty - e.h / 2;
591	    e.spin = 0; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
592	    if (e.ai1 <= 15) {                      // :19278-19292 聚能尘(粒子近似)
593	      game.spawnParticles(
594	        e.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 30, e.cy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 30,
595	        '#C8A040', 1, 0.8, { life: 26 });
596	    }
597	    if (e.ai1 >= 30) {
598	      x.noTC = true;
599	      x.state = 2; e.ai1 = 0;
600	      // 冲拳速度(:19302-19321):12 + 残血/本体受损加成,×(num+3)/4 钳 48
601	      let num6 = 12;
602	      if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 2) num6 += 4;
603	      if (e.hp < e.maxHp / 4) num6 += 4;
604	      if (anchor.hp < anchor.maxHp) num6 += 10;
605	      num6 *= (num + 3) / 4;
606	      if (num6 > 48) num6 = 48;
607	      const pdx = player.cx - e.cx, pdy = player.cy - e.cy;
608	      const pd = Math.hypot(pdx, pdy) || 1;
609	      e.vx = (pdx / pd) * num6; e.vy = (pdy / pd) * num6;
610	      // 冲拳朝向(:19325-19328):默认 atan2(vy,vx),247 镜像为 atan2(-vy,-vx)(与归位相反)
611	      e.spin = isLeft ? Math.atan2(-e.vy, -e.vx) : Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx);
612	    }
613	  } else if (x.state === 2) {
614	    // ---- 冲拳飞行(:19333-19393) ----
615	    e.ai1 += 1;
616	    if (e.ai1 === 1) game.playSfx('roar', 0.4);   // SoundID.Item14(:19344-19346)
617	    if (e.aiT % 2 === 0) {                        // :19348-19356 焰尾尘(粒子近似)
618	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx + e.vx * 0.5, e.cy + e.vy * 0.5, '#E08040', 1, 0.6, { life: 20 });
619	    }
620	    // 过轴判定(:19363-19388):主轴冲过玩家 → 关穿墙(开始撞墙)
621	    if (Math.abs(e.vx) > Math.abs(e.vy)) {
622	      if (e.vx > 0 && e.cx > player.cx) x.noTC = false;
623	      if (e.vx < 0 && e.cx < player.cx) x.noTC = false;
624	    } else {
625	      if (e.vy > 0 && e.cy > player.cy) x.noTC = false;
626	      if (e.vy < 0 && e.cy < player.cy) x.noTC = false;
627	    }
628	    // 离锚 700 → 回位(:19389-19392;撞墙分支在位移后判定)
629	    if (dist > 700) { x.noTC = true; x.state = 0; }
630	  } else if (x.state === 3) {
631	    // ---- 直飞追击(:19394-19446;原版无处置 ai[0]=3 的代码,保留 1:1) ----
632	    x.noTC = true;
633	    const pdx = player.cx - e.cx, pdy = player.cy - e.cy;
634	    const pd = Math.hypot(pdx, pdy) || 1;
635	    e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, (pdx / pd) * 12, 0.4);
636	    e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, (pdy / pd) * 12, 0.4);
637	    e.spin = isLeft ? Math.atan2(-e.vy, -e.vx) : Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx);
638	  }
639	
640	  // ---- 位移(蓄力态已整帧钉锚;冲拳非穿墙段撞墙即回位) ----
641	  if (x.state !== 1) {
642	    e.x += e.vx;
643	    e.y += e.vy;
644	    if (x.state === 2 && !x.noTC && hitSolid(game, e)) {
645	      x.noTC = true;
646	      x.state = 0;                          // :19389-19392 collideX/collideY → 回位
647	    }
648	  }
649	}
650	

```

```
70	function groundGapPx(game: GameHooks, e: Enemy): number {

76	  if (solidAt(tx, ty)) return TILE;

80	    n++;

211	    fly(e);
212	    return;
213	  }
214	
215	  if (e.ai0 === 0) {
216	    // ---- 巡航攻击循环（:36390-36634）----
217	    const w1 = WIN(e.ai3);
218	    e.ai3 += 1;
219	    if (e.ai3 >= 600) e.ai3 = 0;
220	    const w = WIN(e.ai3);
221	    if (w !== w1) {                                // 窗口切换初始化（:36414-36430）
222	      if (w === 0) e.ai2 = 0;
223	      if (w === 1) e.ai2 = player && player.cx > center.x ? 1 : -1;
224	      if (w === 2) e.ai2 = 0;
225	    }
226	    const pcx = player ? player.cx : center.x, pcy = player ? player.cy : center.y;
227	    switch (w) {
228	      case 0: {
229	        // 远侧 600px 悬停（:36432-36446）：到点(<50px)跳 [19] 直进横掠
230	        if (e.ai2 === 0) e.ai2 = -600 * sign(center.x - pcx);
231	        let dx = pcx + e.ai2 - center.x, dy = pcy - 250 - center.y;
232	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
233	        if (d < 50) { e.ai3 = 19; break; }
234	        dx /= d; dy /= d;
235	        e.vx = lerp(e.vx, dx * 16, 0.1); e.vy = lerp(e.vy, dy * 16, 0.1);
236	        break;
237	      }
238	      case 1: {
239	        // 横掠（:36448-36480）：vx=3.5*ai[2];脚下净空<250px 上浮补齐
240	        const gap = groundGapPx(game, e);
241	        if (gap < 250) {
242	          const t = 4 > gap ? -gap : -4;
243	          e.vy = lerp(e.vy, t, 0.05);
244	        } else e.vy *= 0.95;
245	        e.vx = 3.5 * e.ai2;
246	        break;
247	      }
248	      case 2: {
249	        // 归位（:36514-36553）：玩家上方 ±300px;净空<170 抬升;到点(<70px)跳 [279] 进激光窗
250	        if (e.ai2 === 0) e.ai2 = 300 * sign(center.x - pcx);
251	        let dx = pcx + e.ai2 - center.x, dy = pcy - 170 - center.y;
252	        const gap = groundGapPx(game, e);
253	        if (gap < 170) dy -= 170 - gap;
254	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
255	        if (d < 70) { e.ai3 = 279; break; }
256	        dx /= d; dy /= d;
257	        e.vx = lerp(e.vx, dx * 20, 0.1); e.vy = lerp(e.vy, dy * 20, 0.1);
258	        break;
259	      }
260	      case 3: {
261	        // 激光窗缓漂（:36557-36582）：×0.85 双轴 + 净空<170 上浮
262	        const gap = groundGapPx(game, e);
263	        if (gap < 170) {
264	          const t = 4 > gap ? -gap : -4;
265	          e.vy = lerp(e.vy, t, 0.05);
266	        } else e.vy *= 0.85;
267	        e.vx *= 0.85;
268	        break;
269	      }
270	      case 4: {
271	        // 顶悬（:36591-36602）：到点(<50px)跳 [439] 进火炮窗
272	        let dx = pcx - center.x, dy = pcy - 250 - center.y;
273	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
274	        if (d < 50) { e.ai3 = 439; break; }
275	        dx /= d; dy /= d;
276	        e.vx = lerp(e.vx, dx * 16, 0.1); e.vy = lerp(e.vy, dy * 16, 0.1);
277	        break;
278	      }
279	      default: {
280	        // 火炮窗静止（:36604-36605）：×0.85 双轴
281	        e.vx *= 0.85; e.vy *= 0.85;
282	        break;
283	      }
284	    }
285	  } else if (e.ai0 === 1 && EXPERT) {
286	    // ---- expert 部件全灭旋转过渡（:36636-36668,150t）:本工程不可达 ----
287	    e.vx *= 0.96; e.vy *= 0.96;
288	    e.ai1 += 1;
289	    if (e.ai1 >= 150) { e.ai0 = 2; e.ai1 = 0; e.visAngle = 0; }
290	    else if (e.ai1 < 40) e.visAngle = Math.cos((e.ai1 / 40) * Math.PI * 2) * 0.2;
291	    else if (e.ai1 < 80) e.visAngle = Math.cos((e.ai1 / 20) * Math.PI * 2) * 0.3;
292	    else if (e.ai1 < 120) e.visAngle = Math.cos((e.ai1 / 10) * Math.PI * 2) * 0.4;
293	    else e.visAngle = ((e.ai1 - 120) / 30) * Math.PI * 2;
294	  } else if (e.ai0 === 2 && EXPERT) {
295	    // ---- expert 死光弹幕（:36670-36749,3600t 循环;每 120t 后 60t 为射线窗）----
296	    const wasWin = e.ai3 % 120 >= 60 ? 1 : 0;
297	    e.ai3 += 1;
298	    const inWin = e.ai3 % 120 >= 60 ? 1 : 0;
299	    if (inWin !== wasWin) {
300	      if (inWin === 1) {
301	        e.ai2 = player && player.cx > center.x ? 1 : -1;
302	        shootM(game, e.cx, e.cy, 0, 6, 80, 447, 300, 20);   // Projectile 447 + Item12
303	      }
304	    }
305	    if (e.ai3 >= 3600) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
306	    else if (inWin === 0 && player) {
307	      let dx = player.cx + e.ai2 * 350 - center.x, dy = player.cy - 250 - center.y;
308	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
309	      dx /= d; dy /= d;
310	      e.vx = lerp(e.vx, dx * 16, 0.1); e.vy = lerp(e.vy, dy * 16, 0.1);
311	    } else {
312	      const gap = groundGapPx(game, e);
313	      if (gap < 250) {
314	        const t = 4 > gap ? -gap : -4;
315	        e.vy = lerp(e.vy, t, 0.05);
316	      } else e.vy *= 0.95;
317	      e.vx = 8 * e.ai2;
318	    }
319	    e.visAngle = 0;
320	  }
321	  // ---- 出界钳制 + 消散（:36751-36772）----
322	  const st = game.world.store;
323	  if (e.y < -100 || e.x < -100 || e.y > st.h * TILE + 100 || e.x > st.w * TILE + 100) {
324	    e.x = clamp(e.x, -100, st.w * TILE + 100);
325	    e.y = clamp(e.y, -100, st.h * TILE + 100);
326	    e.dead = true;
327	    return;
328	  }
329	  fly(e);
330	}

332	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
333	// AI_075 飞碟部件 392/393/394 + 骑手 390（NPC.cs:35745-36321;416/492 同构锚表一并接）
334	// 锚点偏移（cs:35766/35813/35820/35827）:390→391 (0,-14);392→395 (0,2) 旋转随体;
335	//   393→395 (±60,+29);394→395 (±49,-13),spriteDirection=±1;416→415 (-spriteDir*10,-30)
336	// 主体消亡（:36288-36299）:390→Transform(382) / 416→Transform(518) / 其余消散
337	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
338	export function martianSaucerPartAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
339	  const id = e.vanillaId ?? 0;
340	  // ---- 荷兰大炮 492（DD2 批 bossAI_dd2.ts：锚船体偏移 cs:35889 + 240t 装填炮击
341	  // cs:36008）——aiStyle 75 分发进本函数,转发后不走飞碟部件路径 ----
342	  if (id === 492) { dutchmanCannonAI(e, game, player); return; }
343	  // ---- 骑手首帧生成坐骑（:35750-35762）----
344	  if ((id === 390 || id === 416) && !e.bInit) {
345	    e.bInit = true;
346	    const mount = spawnPart(game, e, id === 390 ? 391 : 415);
347	    if (mount) e.master = mount;
348	  }
349	  if (!e.bInit && (id === 392 || id === 393 || id === 394)) e.bInit = true;
350	
351	  const mount = e.master;
352	  const mountOk = !!mount && !mount.dead;
353	  if (!mountOk) {
354	    // 主体消亡分支（:36288-36299）
355	    if (id === 390 || id === 416) {
356	      e.transformTo(id === 390 ? 382 : 518, game.world.store);   // Transform → 下车变步兵
357	    } else {
358	      e.dead = true;   // 部件随主体消散（life=0 + active=false,无掉落）
359	    }
360	    return;
361	  }
362	  const m = mount as Enemy;
363	  // ---- 锚定跟随（:35764-35845 + 36200-36214）：零速贴主体 + 偏移 ----
364	  let ox = 0, oy = 0;
365	  let spriteDir = m.facing;
366	  if (id === 390) { ox = 0; oy = -14; }
367	  else if (id === 392) { ox = 0; oy = 2; }
368	  else if (id === 393) { ox = e.ai1 === 1 ? 60 : -60; oy = 29; }
369	  else if (id === 394) { ox = e.ai1 === 1 ? 49 : -49; oy = -13; spriteDir = e.ai1 === 1 ? 1 : -1; }
370	  else if (id === 416) { ox = -m.facing * 10; oy = -30; }
371	  e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
372	  e.x = m.cx - e.w / 2 + ox;
373	  e.y = m.cy - e.h / 2 + oy;
374	  // spriteDirection（394 按 ai[1] 固定侧,其余随主体;:36206-36212）
375	  e.facing = spriteDir === 1 ? 1 : -1;
376	
377	  if (id === 390) {
378	    // ---- 骑手射击（:36216-36280）：ai[1] 冷却 60t(受击 -30),<700px+LOS 朝向对 → 438 弹 ----
379	    e.vx = m.vx;
380	    if (e.ai1 < 60) e.ai1 += 1;
381	    if (e.justHit) e.ai1 = -30;
382	    if (player && !player.dead) {
383	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
384	      const canHit = losClear(game, e.x, e.y, player.x, player.y);
385	      if (canHit && Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 700) {
386	        if (e.ai1 === 60 && sign(dx) === e.facing) {
387	          e.ai1 = -60;
388	          const oxp = e.cx, oyp = e.cy - 4;
389	          let sx = player.cx - oxp, sy = player.cy - oyp;
390	          sx += rndRange(-50, 51); sy += rndRange(-50, 51);
391	          sx *= rndRange(80, 121) * 0.01; sy *= rndRange(80, 121) * 0.01;
392	          const d = Math.hypot(sx, sy);
393	          if (d > 0.001) { sx = (sx / d) * 7; sy = (sy / d) * 7; }
394	          else { sx = 0; sy = -7; }
395	          shootM(game, oxp, oyp, sx, sy, 30, 438);
396	        }
397	      }
398	    }
399	    return;
400	  }
401	  if (id !== 392 && id !== 393 && id !== 394) return;
402	
403	  // ---- 部件攻击窗（共享 saucer.ai[3] 计数,cs:36128/36171/36235）----
404	  const saucer = m;
405	  e.ai3 = saucer.ai3;
406	  if (id === 394) {
407	    // 火炮窗 [440,580)（:36124-36150）：每 20t 一发 448,朝向 ±22.5° 散布,速 8
408	    if (e.ai3 >= 440 && e.ai3 < 580) {
409	      const n = e.ai3 - 440;
410	      if (n % 20 === 0) {
411	        const dirx = spriteDir;
412	        const delta = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.7853982;   // ±22.5° 散布（:36133-36136）
413	        shootM(game, e.cx + dirx * 36, e.cy + 8,
414	          Math.cos(delta) * 8 * dirx, Math.sin(delta) * 8 * dirx, 50, 448);
415	      }
416	    }
417	    return;
418	  }
419	  if (id === 393) {
420	    // 激光窗 [280,420)（:36152-36197）：窗内每 6t 一发 449 速 16;窗外只瞄准(ai[2] 角)
421	    const inWin = e.ai3 >= 280 && e.ai3 < 420;
422	    if (!inWin && player && !player.dead) {
423	      let dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
424	      if (dy < 0) dy = 0;
425	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
426	      if (d > 0.001) e.ai2 = Math.atan2(dy / d, dx / d);
427	      else e.ai2 = Math.PI / 2;
428	    }
429	    if (inWin) {
430	      const n = e.ai3 - 280;
431	      if (n % 6 === 0) {
432	        const ang = e.ai2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * (0.7853982 / 3);
433	        shootM(game, e.cx, e.cy, Math.cos(ang) * 16, Math.sin(ang) * 16, 35, 449);
434	      }
435	    }
436	    return;
437	  }
438	  // ---- 392 炮体（:36199-36286）----
439	  // 部件全灭检测（:35791-35820）：炮塔/火炮全灭 → 普通模式飞碟 ai0=3 自毁(expert→1)
440	  {
441	    const list = slots.get(e);
442	    const turrets = list ? list.slice(0, 2).filter((p) => !p.dead && p.vanillaId === 393) : [];
443	    const cannons = list ? list.slice(2, 4).filter((p) => !p.dead && p.vanillaId === 394) : [];
444	    let allDead = saucer.ai0 >= 1 || saucer.ai0 < 0 ? false : true;
445	    if (allDead && (turrets.length > 0 || cannons.length > 0)) allDead = false;
446	    if (allDead && saucer.ai0 === 0) {
447	      if (!EXPERT) { saucer.ai0 = 3; saucer.ai1 = 0; saucer.ai2 = 0; saucer.ai3 = 0; }
448	      else { saucer.ai0 = 1; saucer.ai1 = 0; saucer.ai2 = 0; saucer.ai3 = 0; }
449	    }
450	  }
451	  // 死光电报（:36201-36219）：巡航态 ai[3]∈[20,260) 首帧（n==0）→ Projectile 447
452	  if (e.ai3 >= 20 && e.ai3 < 260 && saucer.ai0 === 0) {
453	    if (e.ai3 - 20 === 0) shootM(game, e.cx, e.cy, 0, 5, 50, 447, 300, 20);
454	  }
455	  // 空巢发射器（:36221-36279）：窗口内对已灭槽位按概率补射 450 导弹
456	  {
457	    const list = slots.get(e) ?? [];
458	    const t0 = list[0], t1 = list[1], c0 = list[2], c1 = list[3];
459	    const t0Dead = !t0 || t0.dead || t0.vanillaId !== 393;
460	    const t1Dead = !t1 || t1.dead || t1.vanillaId !== 393;
461	    const c0Dead = !c0 || c0.dead || c0.vanillaId !== 394;
462	    const c1Dead = !c1 || c1.dead || c1.vanillaId !== 394;
463	    let flag76 = false, mv4 = 1000, mv5 = 1000;
464	    if (saucer.ai0 === 2) { flag76 = true; mv4 = 120; mv5 = 120; }
465	    if (!flag76 && e.ai3 >= 280 && e.ai3 < 400) { flag76 = true; mv5 = 90; mv4 = 60; }
466	    if (!flag76 && e.ai3 >= 440 && e.ai3 < 580) { flag76 = true; mv5 = 60; mv4 = 90; }
467	    if (flag76) {
468	      // 炮塔空巢（:36243-36258）:下方侧向抛射
469	      if (t0Dead && rnd(mv4) === 0) {
470	        const ang = Math.atan2(1, -Math.random() * 3) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.7853982;
471	        shootM(game, e.cx - rndRange(50, 70), e.cy + rndRange(30, 45),
472	          Math.cos(ang) * 3, Math.sin(ang) * 3, 30, 450);
473	      }
474	      if (t1Dead && rnd(mv4) === 0) {
475	        const ang = Math.atan2(1, Math.random() * 3) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.7853982;
476	        shootM(game, e.cx + rndRange(50, 70), e.cy + rndRange(30, 45),
477	          Math.cos(ang) * 3, Math.sin(ang) * 3, 30, 450);
478	      }
479	      // 火炮空巢（:36261-36276）:上方侧向抛射
480	      if (c0Dead && rnd(mv5) === 0) {
481	        const ang = Math.atan2(-1, -Math.random() * 2) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.7853982;
482	        shootM(game, e.cx - rndRange(30, 60), e.cy + rndRange(-30, -10),
483	          Math.cos(ang) * 3, Math.sin(ang) * 3, 30, 450);
484	      }
485	      if (c1Dead && rnd(mv5) === 0) {
486	        const ang = Math.atan2(-1, Math.random() * 2) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.7853982;
487	        shootM(game, e.cx + rndRange(30, 60), e.cy + rndRange(-30, -10),
488	          Math.cos(ang) * 3, Math.sin(ang) * 3, 30, 450);
489	      }
490	    }
491	  }
492	}

494	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
495	// AI_074 火星无人机 388 + 日耀 Corite 418（NPC.cs:35452-35744,参数表 :35477-35509）
496	// ai[0] 0=悬浮瞄准(LOS+俯角+距离带 → 锁方向) / 1=蓄力(×0.95,30t) / 2=扑咬(steer 追击) /
497	//        3=自爆(388 专属:64px 贴身或撞块;3t 后炸) / 4=418 扑后恢复
498	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
499	export function martianDroneAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
500	  const corite = e.vanillaId === 418;
501	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
502	  // 参数表（:35477-35509）：388 / 418
503	  const kbMult = corite ? 0.3 : 0.4;
504	  const spd = corite ? 8 : 10;
505	  const hover = corite ? 175 : 200;
506	  const near = corite ? 175 : 0;
507	  const far = corite ? 600 : 750;
508	  const minNear = corite ? 80 : 0;
509	  const accel = corite ? 60 : 30;
510	  const windup = corite ? 20 : 30;
511	  const decay = corite ? 0.75 : 0.95;
512	  const jitter = corite ? 0 : 50;
513	  const dashSpd = corite ? 9 : 14;
514	  const steerN = corite ? 60 : 20;
515	  const steerAcc = corite ? (4 / 15) * 60 : 0;
516	  const minSpd = 7;
517	  const canExplode = !corite;   // flag68（:35491）
518	
519	  // 朝向（:35454-35469）
520	  if (sign(e.vx) !== 0) e.facing = corite ? sign(e.vx) : -sign(e.vx);
521	
522	  if (e.ai0 === 0 && player && !player.dead) {
523	    // ---- 悬浮瞄准（:35540-35610）----
524	    e.def.knockbackResist = kbMult;    // GetKnockbackMultiplier_ScaledByDifficulty
525	    const dx0 = player.cx - e.cx, dy0 = player.cy - e.cy;
526	    const dist = Math.hypot(dx0, dy0);
527	    // 悬停航向（:35546-35552）：直连向量 - 上方 hover - 侧移 near,归一 × spd
528	    const hx = dx0 + (dx0 < 0 ? near : -near), hy = dy0 - hover;
529	    const hd = Math.hypot(hx, hy) || 1;
530	    const vxT = (hx / hd) * spd, vyT = (hy / hd) * spd;
531	    let canHit = losClear(game, e.cx, e.cy, player.cx, player.cy);
532	    if (e.ai3 >= 120) canHit = true;                     // :35556-35558
533	    const aimAng = Math.atan2(dy0, dx0);
534	    const angleOk = aimAng > Math.PI / 8 && aimAng < Math.PI - Math.PI / 8;
535	    const tooNear = dist < minNear, tooFar = dist > far;
536	    if (tooNear || tooFar || !canHit || !angleOk) {
537	      // 悬停逼近（:35564-35583）
538	      e.vx = (e.vx * (accel - 1) + vxT) / accel;
539	      e.vy = (e.vy * (accel - 1) + vyT) / accel;
540	      if (!canHit) {
541	        if (!tooNear && !tooFar) e.ai3 += 1;
542	      } else e.ai3 = 0;
543	    } else {
544	      // 锁定扑咬方向（:35585-35591）
545	      e.ai0 = 1; e.ai1 = 0;
546	      e.ai2 = (dx0 / (dist || 1)) * spd;
547	      e.ai3 = (dy0 / (dist || 1)) * spd;
548	    }
549	  } else if (e.ai0 === 1) {
550	    // ---- 蓄力（:35592-35617）----
551	    e.def.knockbackResist = 0;
552	    let keep = true;
553	    if (corite) {
554	      keep = Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) > 2;
555	      if (!keep && player && !player.dead) {
556	        const d = Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) || 1;
557	        e.vx = lerp(e.vx, ((player.cx - e.cx) / d) * 0.1, 0.25);
558	        e.vy = lerp(e.vy, ((player.cy - e.cy) / d) * 0.1, 0.25);
559	      }
560	    }
561	    if (keep) { e.vx *= decay; e.vy *= decay; }
562	    e.ai1 += 1;
563	    if (e.ai1 >= windup) {
564	      e.ai0 = 2; e.ai1 = 0;
565	      let dx = e.ai2 + rndRange(-jitter, jitter + 1) * 0.04;
566	      let dy = e.ai3 + rndRange(-jitter, jitter + 1) * 0.04;
567	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
568	      dx = (dx / (d || 1)) * dashSpd; dy = (dy / (d || 1)) * dashSpd;
569	      e.vx = dx; e.vy = dy;
570	    }
571	  } else if (e.ai0 === 2) {
572	    // ---- 扑咬（:35618-35659）----
573	    e.def.knockbackResist = 0;
574	    e.ai1 += 1;
575	    const farBelow = player && !player.dead
576	      && Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) > (corite ? 150 : 100)
577	      && e.cy > player.cy;
578	    if ((e.ai1 >= 30 && farBelow) || Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy) < minSpd) {
579	      e.ai0 = corite ? 4 : 0; e.ai1 = corite ? 45 : 0;
580	      e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
581	      e.vx /= 2; e.vy /= 2;
582	    } else if (player) {
583	      const d = Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) || 1;
584	      const ux = (player.cx - e.cx) / d, uy = (player.cy - e.cy) / d;
585	      const spdNow = Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy);
586	      e.vx = (e.vx * (steerN - 1) + ux * (spdNow + steerAcc)) / steerN;
587	      e.vy = (e.vy * (steerN - 1) + uy * (spdNow + steerAcc)) / steerN;
588	    }
589	    // 撞块自爆（:35650-35656,仅 388）
590	    if (canExplode) {
591	      const st = game.world.store;
592	      const x0 = Math.floor(e.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((e.x + e.w - 0.01) / TILE);
593	      const y0 = Math.floor(e.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((e.y + e.h - 0.01) / TILE);
594	      outer: for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
595	        for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
596	          if (tx >= 0 && ty >= 0 && tx < st.w && ty < st.h && st.isSolid(tx, ty)) {
597	            e.ai0 = 3; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;
598	            break outer;
599	          }
600	        }
601	      }
602	    }
603	  } else if (e.ai0 === 4) {

605	    e.ai1 -= 3;
606	    if (e.ai1 <= 0) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }

614	  if (e.ai0 === 3) {

637	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
638	// AI_073 特斯拉炮塔 387（NPC.cs:35340-35451）
639	// ai[1] <120 = 部署（60-120t 渐显,alpha 255→0,部署期无敌）;ai[0] 电击计时:
640	//   <60 递增,受击 -30;==60 → 放电 435(伤 35,速 14,±100px 抖动) 并置 -120 → 180t 循环
641	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
642	export function teslaTurretAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
643	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }   // 原版 ai[0] 自 0 起 → 首射 60t
644	  if (player && !player.dead) e.facing = player.cx >= e.cx ? 1 : -1;
645	  // 地面摩擦（:35343-35349）
646	  e.vx *= 0.93;
647	  if (e.vx > -0.1 && e.vx < 0.1) e.vx = 0;
648	
649	  if (e.ai1 < 120) {
650	    // ---- 部署（:35353-35401）：渐显 + 部署期无敌 + 环绕尘 ----
651	    e.ai1 += 1;
652	    e.spawnAlpha = e.ai1 > 60 ? Math.round((1 - (e.ai1 - 60) / 60) * 255) : 255;
653	    e.iframes = 2;                       // dontTakeDamage = true
654	    if (e.ai1 % 6 === 0) {
655	      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#6FD7FF', 1, 0.6, { life: 20 });
656	    }
657	    e.lightRGB = [0.1 * (e.ai1 / 60), 0.5 * (e.ai1 / 60), 0.7 * (e.ai1 / 60)];
658	    e.vy = Math.min(e.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
659	    moveAndCollide(e, game.world, e.vx, e.vy);
660	    return;
661	  }
662	  if (e.ai1 === 120) e.ai1 += 1;         // :35403-35406 单帧过门
663	  e.lightRGB = [0.1, 0.5, 0.7];
664	
665	  // ---- 电击循环（:35409-35450）----
666	  if (e.ai0 < 60) e.ai0 += 1;
667	  if (e.justHit) e.ai0 = -30;            // 受击打断充电
668	  if (e.ai0 === 60 && player && !player.dead) {
669	    e.ai0 = -120;
670	    const ox = e.cx, oy = e.cy - 10;
671	    let dx = player.cx - ox, dy = player.cy - oy;
672	    dx += rndRange(-100, 101); dy += rndRange(-100, 101);
673	    dx *= rndRange(70, 131) * 0.01; dy *= rndRange(70, 131) * 0.01;
674	    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
675	    if (d > 0.001) { dx = (dx / d) * 14; dy = (dy / d) * 14; }
676	    else { dx = 0; dy = -14; }
677	    shootM(game, ox, oy, dx, dy, 35, 435);
678	  }
679	  e.vy = Math.min(e.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
680	  moveAndCollide(e, game.world, e.vx, e.vy);
681	}

686	export function bubbleShieldAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, _player: Player | null) {
687	  void game; void _player;
688	  const owner = e.master;
689	  if (owner && !owner.dead && owner.vanillaId === 383) {
690	    e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
691	    e.x = owner.cx - e.w / 2;
692	    e.y = owner.cy - e.h / 2;
693	    e.lightRGB = [0.1, 0.5, 0.7];

700	// AI_049 愤怒云 250（NPC.cs:31742-31810,普查同批顺带）
701	// 悬顶 200px 伺服（accel 0.25,过零双倍）;正下方+LOS → ai[0] 累计,>8 淋一发雨 264
702	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
703	export function angryNimbusAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
704	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; }
705	  if (!player || player.dead) {
706	    e.vx *= 0.95; e.vy *= 0.95;
707	    moveAndCollide(e, game.world, e.vx, e.vy);
708	    return;
709	  }
710	  const ACC = 0.25;
711	  let tx = player.cx - e.cx, ty = player.cy - 200 - e.cy;
712	  const d = Math.hypot(tx, ty);
713	  if (d < 20) { tx = e.vx; ty = e.vy; }
714	  else { const s = 4 / d; tx *= s; ty *= s; }
715	  // 分轴伺服（:31758-31789,过零方向翻转时双倍加速）
716	  if (e.vx < tx) { e.vx += ACC; if (e.vx < 0 && tx > 0) e.vx += ACC * 2; }
717	  else if (e.vx > tx) { e.vx -= ACC; if (e.vx > 0 && tx < 0) e.vx -= ACC * 2; }
718	  if (e.vy < ty) { e.vy += ACC; if (e.vy < 0 && ty > 0) e.vy += ACC * 2; }
719	  else if (e.vy > ty) { e.vy -= ACC; if (e.vy > 0 && ty < 0) e.vy -= ACC * 2; }
720	  // 淋雨（:31791-31808）：水平重叠 + 云底在玩家上方 + LOS → 8t 一发
721	  if (e.x + e.w > player.x && e.x < player.x + player.w
722	    && e.y + e.h < player.y
723	    && losClear(game, e.x, e.y, player.x, player.y)) {
724	    e.ai0 += 1;
725	    if (e.ai0 > 8) {
726	      e.ai0 = 0;
727	      const ox = e.x + 10 + Math.random() * (e.w - 20);
728	      shootM(game, ox, e.y + e.h + 4, 0, 5, 20, 264);
729	    }
730	  }
731	  moveAndCollide(e, game.world, e.vx, e.vy);
732	}

739	  void player;
740	  const id = e.vanillaId;

744	    const l0 = la(e);
745	    let shield = officerShield.get(e);
746	    const shieldOk = !!shield && !shield.dead && shield.vanillaId === 384;
747	    if (e.ai2 === 0 && l0[0] === 0 && !shieldOk) {
748	      const s = spawnPart(game, e, 384);
749	      if (s) {
750	        shield = s;
751	        officerShield.set(e, shield);
752	        shield.master = e;          // 原版盾 ai[0] = 军官 index（:56621）
753	        e.ai2 = 1;                  // 原版 ai[2] = 盾 index+1（>0 标记）
754	        l0[0] = -1;
755	      }
756	    }
757	    if (shieldOk) {
758	      e.iframes = 2;                // dontTakeDamage = true（:56629）
759	    } else {
760	      e.ai2 = 0;
761	      if (l0[0] === -1) l0[0] = 180;
762	      if (l0[0] > 0) l0[0] -= 1;
763	    }
764	    return;
765	  }
766	  if (id === 386) {
767	    // ---- 工程师：放炮塔（:59384-59428）----
768	    if (e.confusedT > 0) { e.ai2 = -60; return; }
769	    if (e.ai2 < 60) e.ai2 += 1;
770	    const nT = enemies.filter((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 387).length;
771	    const nE = enemies.filter((n) => !n.dead && n.vanillaId === 386).length;
772	    if (e.ai2 > 0 && nT >= 4 * nE) e.ai2 = 0;      // 场上 387 ≥ 4×386（:59393-59396）
773	    if (e.justHit) e.ai2 = -30;                    // :59397-59400
774	    if (e.ai2 === 30) {
775	      // ±5t 随机扫位（:59402-59424）：非中心 2t 禁区 + 实心面 + 上方 4t 净空 + 无岩浆
776	      const st = game.world.store;
777	      const cx0 = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE), cy0 = Math.floor(e.y / TILE);
778	      for (let tries = 0; tries < 100; tries++) {
779	        const tx = rndRange(cx0 - 5, cx0 + 5);
780	        const tyTop = rndRange(cy0 - 5, cy0 + 5);
781	        for (let ty = tyTop; ty < cy0 + 5; ty++) {
782	          const inCore = Math.abs(ty - cy0) <= 2 && Math.abs(tx - cx0) <= 2;
783	          const inSpawn = Math.abs(ty - cy0) <= 0 && Math.abs(tx - cx0) <= 0;
784	          if (inCore || inSpawn) continue;
785	          if (tx < 1 || ty < 1 || tx >= st.w - 1 || ty >= st.h - 1) continue;
786	          if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty)) continue;
787	          if (st.liquid[st.idx(tx, ty - 1)] === 2) continue;   // 上面一格岩浆
788	          let clear = true;
789	          for (let k = 1; k <= 4; k++) if (st.isSolid(tx, ty - k)) { clear = false; break; }
790	          if (!clear) continue;
791	          const turret = spawnPart(game, e, 387);
792	          if (turret) {
793	            turret.x = tx * TILE + 8 - turret.w / 2;
794	            turret.y = ty * TILE - turret.h;   // 底边贴实心面顶（:59425-59426）
795	          }
796	          return;
797	        }
798	      }
799	    }
800	    if (e.ai2 === 60) e.ai2 = -120;                 // :59427-59429
801	  }
802	}
```

```
224	      let dx = player.cx - ox, dy = player.y - oy;
225	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
226	      const s = 16 / d;
227	      dx *= s * (1 + rndRange(-20, 21) * 0.001);
228	      dy *= s * (1 + rndRange(-20, 21) * 0.001);
229	      shootE(game, ox, oy, dx, dy, 75, 325);
230	    }
231	    if (e.ai1 >= 120) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai0 = 0; }
232	  } else if (e.ai0 === 4 && player) {
233	    // 325 专属希腊火连泼(:33146-33179)：每 10t 一发 326-328（伤 50 速 12→16），240t 收招
234	    num891 = 4;
235	    e.ai1 += 1;
236	    if (e.ai1 % 10 === 0) {
237	      let spd = 12;
238	      const ox = e.cx, oy = e.cy + 30;
239	      let dx = player.cx - ox, dy = player.y - oy;
240	      dy -= Math.abs(dx) * 0.2;
241	      spd += Math.abs(dx) * 0.002;
242	      if (spd > 16) spd = 16;
243	      dx += rndRange(-50, 51); dy -= rndRange(50, 201);
244	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
245	      const s = spd / d;
246	      dx *= s * (1 + rndRange(-30, 31) * 0.005);
247	      dy *= s * (1 + rndRange(-30, 31) * 0.005);
248	      shootE(game, ox, oy, dx, dy, 50, rndRange(326, 329));
249	    }
250	    if (e.ai1 >= 240) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai0 = 0; }
251	  }
252	  // 玩家在正下方 50px 内 → 停步(:33180-33185)
253	  if (player && Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx) < 50) flag56 = true;
254	  hoverCruise(e, game, player, flag56, num891);
255	}
256	
257	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
258	// AI_058 南瓜王 327（NPC.cs:33246-33404）
259	// localAI[0/1]=表情帧节拍；localAI[2]=300t 换招计时 → ai[3] = rand(3)
260	//   （0 希腊火散射 / 1 悬浮→俯冲 / 2 交由双刃放焰镰）。
261	// ai[0]=0 首帧生双刃 328（ai0=-1/1 侧别）；ai[1] 0=悬浮(300t) 1=俯冲(16 速) 2=离场。
262	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
263	
264	/** 刃 → 南瓜王（原版 npc[ai[1]]；先走 master 引用，断链回退 id 查找） */
265	function findPumpking(game: GameHooks, e: Enemy): Enemy | null {
266	  if (e.master && !e.master.dead && e.master.vanilla?.aiStyle === 58) return e.master;
267	  return (game.enemies() as Enemy[]).find(
268	    (n) => !n.dead && n.id === e.ai1 && n.vanilla?.aiStyle === 58) ?? null;
269	}
270	
271	export function pumpkingAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
272	  const L = la(e);
273	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; L[2] = 0; }
274	  // 表情帧节拍(:33247-33256)：6t 一拍，5 拍一循环（渲染帧端消费）
275	  L[0]++;
276	  if (L[0] > 6) { L[0] = 0; L[1]++; if (L[1] > 4) L[1] = 0; }
277	  if (e.vanillaId === 327) e.lightRGB = [0.4, 0.4, 0.2];   // :33262-33264
278	  // 换招(:33266-33272)
279	  L[2]++;
280	  if (L[2] > 300) {
281	    L[2] = 0;
282	    e.ai3 = rnd(3);
283	  } else if (e.ai3 === 0 && L[2] % 30 === 0 && L[2] > 30 && player) {
284	    // 希腊火散射(:33273-33298)：每 30t 从 (cx, cy+30) 一发 326-328（伤 40 速 5，
285	    // 出膛点须非实心格）；dy*0.2 = 近乎水平的扇面散射
286	    const ox = e.cx, oy = e.cy + 30;
287	    if (!solidTileAt(game, ox, oy)) {
288	      let dx = player.cx - ox, dy = player.y - oy;
289	      dx += rndRange(-50, 51);
290	      dy += rndRange(50, 201);
291	      dy *= 0.2;
292	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
293	      const s = 5 / d;
294	      dx *= s * (1 + rndRange(-30, 31) * 0.01);
295	      dy *= s * (1 + rndRange(-30, 31) * 0.01);
296	      shootE(game, ox, oy, dx, dy, 40, rndRange(326, 329));
297	    }
298	  }
299	  // 首帧生双刃(:33300-33316)：ai0=-1 左 / +1 右（右刃 ai3=150 预蓄）
300	  if (e.ai0 === 0) {
301	    if (player) e.facing = e.seekDirX(player);
302	    e.ai0 = 1;
303	    for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
304	      const b = spawnPart(game, e, 328);
305	      if (!b) continue;
306	      b.ai0 = side;
307	      b.ai1 = e.id;
308	      b.ai2 = 0;
309	      b.ai3 = side === 1 ? 150 : 0;
310	      b.master = e;
311	    }
312	  }
313	  // 玩家死/2000px 外 → 离场态(:33318-33331)
314	  const gone = !player || player.dead
315	    || Math.abs(e.x - player.x) > 2000 || Math.abs(e.y - player.y) > 2000;
316	  if (gone && e.ai1 !== 2) e.ai1 = 2;
317	
318	  if (game.world.clock.isDay) {
319	    // 白天坠离(:33333-33336)
320	    e.vy += 0.3;
321	    e.vx *= 0.9;
322	  } else if (e.ai1 === 0) {
323	    // 悬浮(:33337-33385)：伺服玩家上方 200px；选了俯冲招(ai3==1)且蓄满 300t → 转俯冲
324	    e.ai2++;
325	    if (e.ai2 >= 300) {
326	      if (e.ai3 !== 1) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
327	      else {
328	        e.ai1 = 1; e.ai2 = 0;
329	        if (player) e.facing = e.seekDirX(player);
330	      }
331	    }
332	    if (player) {
333	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - 200 - e.cy;
334	      let dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
335	      let spd = 6;
336	      if (e.ai3 === 1) {           // 俯冲招蓄力期赶路提速(:33358-33370)
337	        if (dist > 900) spd = 12;
338	        else if (dist > 600) spd = 10;
339	        else if (dist > 300) spd = 8;
340	      }
341	      if (dist > 50) {
342	        const k = spd / dist;
343	        e.vx = (e.vx * 14 + dx * k) / 15;
344	        e.vy = (e.vy * 14 + dy * k) / 15;
345	      }
346	    }
347	  } else if (e.ai1 === 1) {
348	    // 俯冲(:33386-33400)：16 速直扑玩家中心；600t 或换招后回悬浮
349	    e.ai2 += 1;
350	    if (e.ai2 >= 600 || e.ai3 !== 1) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }
351	    if (player) {
352	      const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
353	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
354	      const k = 16 / d;
355	      e.vx = (e.vx * 49 + dx * k) / 50;
356	      e.vy = (e.vy * 49 + dy * k) / 50;
357	    }
358	  } else if (e.ai1 === 2) {
359	    // 离场(:33401-33404... 原行号 :33386 段内)
360	    e.vy += 0.1;
361	    if (e.vy < 0) e.vy *= 0.95;
362	    e.vx *= 0.95;
363	    e.encourageDespawn(500);
364	  }
365	  e.visAngle = e.vx * -0.02;       // rotation(:33402)
366	  e.x += e.vx;                     // noTileCollide（SetDefaults 327）直积分
367	  e.y += e.vy;
368	}
369	
370	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
371	// AI_059 南瓜王之刃 328（NPC.cs:33405-33587）
372	// ai[0]=侧别(-1/1)；ai[1]=主 id；ai[2] 0/3=环绕 →1=升空 →2=俯冲 →(回 3) /
373	//        →4=横摆 →5=横扫回环；ai[3]=环绕计时。主选焰镰招(ai[3]==2)时 90t 一发 329。
374	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
375	
376	export function pumpkingBladeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
377	  const L = la(e);
378	  const side = e.ai0 < 0 ? -1 : 1;                 // num937(:33407)
379	  e.facing = -side;                                 // spriteDirection(:33410)
380	  // 主缺失/非 aiStyle 58 → 自毁(:33412-33427)
381	  const master = findPumpking(game, e);
382	  if (!master) {
383	    e.vx *= 0.9; e.vy *= 0.9;
384	    e.dead = true;
385	    return;
386	  }
387	  e.master = master;
388	  // 主选焰镰招(:33429-33447)：90t 一发焰镰 329（伤 60，0.01/距离 的低速追踪弹）
389	  if (master.ai3 === 2 && player) {
390	    L[1]++;
391	    if (L[1] > 90) {
392	      L[1] = 0;
393	      const ox = e.cx, oy = e.cy + 30;
394	      const dx = player.cx - ox, dy = player.y - oy;
395	      const d = Math.max(1, Math.hypot(dx, dy));
396	      const k = 0.01 / d;
397	      shootE(game, e.cx, e.cy, dx * k, dy * k, 60, 329);
398	    }
399	  }
400	
401	  if (game.world.clock.isDay) {
402	    // 白天坠离(:33449-33452)
403	    e.vy += 0.3;
404	    e.vx *= 0.9;
405	  } else if (e.ai2 === 0 || e.ai2 === 3) {
406	    // 环绕(:33454-33506)：目标=玩家/主中点偏 (-170*side, +90)；玩家主相距 >700
407	    // 则退回主旁；按距离 6/9/12/15/18/21 速伺服；180t → 升下一态
408	    if (master.ai1 === 2) e.encourageDespawn(10);
409	    e.ai3++;
410	    if (e.ai3 >= 180) { e.ai2 += 1; e.ai3 = 0; }
411	    let tx: number, ty: number;
412	    if (player) {
413	      tx = (player.cx + master.cx) / 2 - 170 * side - e.cx;
414	      ty = (player.cy + master.cy) / 2 + 90 - e.cy;
415	      const manh = Math.abs(player.cx - master.cx) + Math.abs(player.cy - master.cy);
416	      if (manh > 700) { tx = master.cx - 170 * side - e.cx; ty = master.cy + 90 - e.cy; }
417	    } else {
418	      tx = master.cx - 170 * side - e.cx;
419	      ty = master.cy + 90 - e.cy;
420	    }
421	    let dist = Math.max(1, Math.hypot(tx, ty));
422	    let spd = 6;
423	    if (dist > 1000) spd = 21;
424	    else if (dist > 800) spd = 18;
425	    else if (dist > 600) spd = 15;
426	    else if (dist > 400) spd = 12;
427	    else if (dist > 200) spd = 9;
428	    // 侧别推离：把刃往主外侧推 4px/t(:33492-33497)
429	    if (side < 0 && e.cx > master.cx) tx -= 4;
430	    if (side > 0 && e.cx < master.cx) tx += 4;
431	    const k = spd / dist;
432	    e.vx = (e.vx * 14 + tx * k) / 15;
433	    e.vy = (e.vy * 14 + ty * k) / 15;
434	    dist = Math.max(1, Math.hypot(tx, ty));
435	    if (dist > 20) e.visAngle = Math.atan2(ty, tx) + Math.PI / 2;   // :33502-33505
436	  } else if (e.ai2 === 1) {
437	    // 升空(:33508-33534)：退到主外侧 200px、上方 230px；升过主顶 200px →
438	    // 18 速直扑玩家转俯冲
439	    const tx = master.cx - 200 * side - e.cx;
440	    const ty = master.y + 230 - e.cy;
441	    e.visAngle = Math.atan2(ty, tx) + Math.PI / 2;
442	    e.vx *= 0.95;
443	    e.vy -= 0.3;
444	    if (e.vy < -14) e.vy = -14;
445	    if (e.y < master.y - 200) {
446	      e.ai2 = 2;
447	      if (player) {
448	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
449	        const d = Math.max(1, Math.hypot(dx, dy));
450	        const k = 18 / d;
451	        e.vx = dx * k;
452	        e.vy = dy * k;
453	      }
454	    }
455	  } else if (e.ai2 === 2) {
456	    // 俯冲(:33536-33546)：越过玩家高度/开始上飘/离主 800px → 回环绕
457	    const manh = Math.abs(e.cx - master.cx) + Math.abs(e.cy - master.cy);
458	    if (!player || e.y > player.y || e.vy < 0 || manh > 800) e.ai2 = 3;
459	  } else if (e.ai2 === 4) {
460	    // 横摆(:33548-33574)：钉主外侧 200px/上 230px，横速朝主对侧 ±14；
461	    // 离主 500px → 17 速直扑玩家转横扫
462	    const tx = master.cx - 200 * side - e.cx;
463	    const ty = master.y + 230 - e.cy;
464	    e.visAngle = Math.atan2(ty, tx) + Math.PI / 2;
465	    e.vy *= 0.95;
466	    e.vx += 0.3 * -side;
467	    if (e.vx < -14) e.vx = -14;
468	    if (e.vx > 14) e.vx = 14;
469	    if (e.cx < master.cx - 500 || e.cx > master.cx + 500) {
470	      e.ai2 = 5;
471	      if (player) {
472	        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
473	        const d = Math.max(1, Math.hypot(dx, dy));
474	        const k = 17 / d;
475	        e.vx = dx * k;
476	        e.vy = dy * k;
477	      }
478	    }
479	  } else if (e.ai2 === 5) {
480	    // 横扫回环(:33576-33586)：越过玩家水平轴/离主 800px → 回环绕
481	    const manh = Math.abs(e.cx - master.cx) + Math.abs(e.cy - master.cy);
482	    if (!player || (e.vx > 0 && e.cx > player.cx) || (e.vx < 0 && e.cx < player.cx) || manh > 800) {
483	      e.ai2 = 0;
484	    }
485	  }
486	  e.x += e.vx;                     // noTileCollide（SetDefaults 328）直积分
487	  e.y += e.vy;
488	}
489	
490	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
491	// AI_060 冰雪女王 345（NPC.cs:33588-33901）
492	// ai[0] -1=重选 / 0=滑翔+FrostWave 348 / 1=压制+FrostShard 349 落冰 /
493	//        2=自旋+FrostShard 349 环射。ai[1]=rand(1,4)/t 蓄招；ai[2]=滑翔朝向；
494	// ai[3]=弹幕节拍（0/负计数半周期）。残血四档全面提速（75/50/25/10%）。
495	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
496	
497	export function iceQueenAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
498	  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0; }
499	  if (game.world.clock.isDay) {
500	    // 白天飞离(:33590-33599)：沿当前水平向加速 + 缓升
501	    e.vx += e.vx > 0 ? 0.25 : -0.25;
502	    e.vy -= 0.1;
503	    e.visAngle = e.vx * 0.05;
504	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
505	    return;
506	  }
507	  const r = e.hp / e.maxHp;
508	  if (!player) {
509	    e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
510	    return;
511	  }
512	  e.facing = e.seekDirX(player);             // TargetClosest（各态首行）
513	
514	  if (e.ai0 === 0) {
515	    // 滑翔(:33601-33686)：固定水平朝向 ai[2]（与玩家同侧），越过 800px 才回摆
516	    if (e.ai2 === 0) e.ai2 = e.cx < player.cx ? 1 : -1;
517	    const dxAbs = Math.abs(e.cx - player.cx);
518	    if (e.cx < player.cx && e.ai2 < 0 && dxAbs > 800) e.ai2 = 0;
519	    if (e.cx > player.cx && e.ai2 > 0 && dxAbs > 800) e.ai2 = 0;
```

```
323	function simpleFlyMove(e: Enemy, dvx: number, dvy: number, acc: number): void {
324	  if (e.vx < dvx) { e.vx += acc; if (e.vx < 0 && dvx > 0) e.vx += acc; }
```

```
368	function ritualCheckFloor(game: GameHooks, cx: number, cy: number): Array<{ x: number; y: number }> | null {
369	  const pts: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];

376	      const floor = solidTileAt(game, x, y) || game.world.store.isPlatform(x, y);
377	      if (!floor) continue;
378	      // 净空:本柱 3 格全空,或(本柱空 且 左右柱上两行空)——原版双条件或语义
379	      const clearMid = !solidRect(game, x - 1, x + 1, y - 3, y - 1);
380	      const clearSide = !solidRect(game, x, x, y - 3, y - 1)
381	        && !solidRect(game, x + 1, x + 1, y - 3, y - 2)
382	        && !solidRect(game, x - 1, x - 1, y - 3, y - 2);
383	      if (clearMid || clearSide) { pts.push({ x, y }); break; }
384	    }
385	  }
386	  return pts.length === 4 ? pts : null;
387	}
```

```
第 8/9 条顺带提示:`(e.ai0 || 1)` 这个写法对负值哨兵**没有**防护意义,若未来有人复用该模式建议改成显式 `(e.ai0 === -1 ? -1 : 1)` 或入口归一。

### 附:spawn 侧负值语义(合法,非 bug,但请留意消费方)

- **bossAI_lategame.ts:1155** `minion.ai0 = -500 * Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)` — 史后仆从(658-660)的故意负预置(且 `Next(3)` 可掷出 0 → ai0=0)。其消费方是**本 10 文件之外**的仆从 AI,请确认那边按"负值爬升"语义消费,否则会出现与本次审计同型的延迟。
- bossAI_dd2.ts:643 / :1164 `e.ai0 = Math.min(e.ai0 + 1, 0)` — 运行中自设负值(:647 置 -300、:668 置 -200、:1205 置 -20/-120)后的冷却回摆,**合法**。
- bossAI_martian.ts:667/:669(tesla `ai0 = -30 / -120`)、bossAI_moon_events.ts:814-818(flocko `ai0 -= 1` 后比 `> 0`)、iceQueen :558/:598/:621 的 -1 — 均为运行中自设,**合法**。
```
